    <script>
        // Bloquear requisições AJAX para domínios externos
        (function() {
            // Domínios externos permitidos
            const allowedDomains = [
                '127.0.0.1',
                'localhost',
                'u6bdxq3r4k.execute-api.us-east-1.amazonaws.com' // AWS API Gateway para ReadMe/Polly
            ];

            function isAllowedDomain(hostname) {
                if (hostname === window.location.hostname) {
                    return true;
                }
                return allowedDomains.includes(hostname);
            }

            // Interceptar XMLHttpRequest
            const originalOpen = XMLHttpRequest.prototype.open;
            XMLHttpRequest.prototype.open = function(method, url, ...args) {
                try {
                    const urlObj = new URL(url, window.location.href);
                    if (!isAllowedDomain(urlObj.hostname)) {
                        console.warn('Bloqueada requisição para domínio externo:', url);
                        return;
                    }
                } catch (e) {
                    // URL relativa, permitir
                }
                return originalOpen.apply(this, [method, url, ...args]);
            };

            // Interceptar jQuery AJAX após jQuery carregar
            if (typeof jQuery !== 'undefined') {
                const originalAjax = jQuery.ajax;
                jQuery.ajax = function(options) {
                    if (options && options.url) {
                        try {
                            const urlObj = new URL(options.url, window.location.href);
                            if (!isAllowedDomain(urlObj.hostname)) {
                                console.warn('Bloqueada requisição jQuery para domínio externo:', options.url);
                                return jQuery.Deferred().reject({
                                    status: 0,
                                    statusText: 'Blocked'
                                });
                            }
                        } catch (e) {
                            // URL relativa, permitir
                        }
                    }
                    return originalAjax.apply(this, arguments);
                };
            } else {
                // Se jQuery ainda não carregou, interceptar depois
                document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
                    if (typeof jQuery !== 'undefined') {
                        const originalAjax = jQuery.ajax;
                        jQuery.ajax = function(options) {
                            if (options && options.url) {
                                try {
                                    const urlObj = new URL(options.url, window.location.href);
                                    if (!isAllowedDomain(urlObj.hostname)) {
                                        console.warn('Bloqueada requisição jQuery para domínio externo:',
                                            options.url);
                                        return jQuery.Deferred().reject({
                                            status: 0,
                                            statusText: 'Blocked'
                                        });
                                    }
                                } catch (e) {
                                    // URL relativa, permitir
                                }
                            }
                            return originalAjax.apply(this, arguments);
                        };
                    }
                });
            }
        })();

        // Funcionalidade do ícone de busca
        (function() {
            const searchIcon = document.querySelector('.search_form_menu_personal_click');
            const searchBox = document.querySelector('.jl_ajse.search_form_menu_personal');
            const searchInput = document.getElementById('search-input') || document.querySelector(
                '#search-form .search_btn');
            const searchForm = document.getElementById('search-form');
            const autocompleteContainer = document.getElementById('search-autocomplete');

            if (searchIcon && searchBox) {
                // Toggle do formulário de busca
                searchIcon.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    e.stopPropagation();

                    // Toggle da classe active
                    const isActive = searchBox.style.display === 'block';
                    searchBox.style.display = isActive ? 'none' : 'block';

                    // Focar no input quando abrir
                    if (!isActive && searchInput) {
                        setTimeout(function() {
                            searchInput.focus();
                        }, 100);
                    }
                });

                // Fechar ao clicar fora
                document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                    if (searchBox && searchBox.style.display === 'block') {
                        if (!searchBox.contains(e.target) && !searchIcon.contains(e.target)) {
                            searchBox.style.display = 'none';
                            if (autocompleteContainer) {
                                autocompleteContainer.style.display = 'none';
                            }
                        }
                    }
                });

                // Fechar ao pressionar ESC
                document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
                    if (e.key === 'Escape' && searchBox && searchBox.style.display === 'block') {
                        searchBox.style.display = 'none';
                        if (autocompleteContainer) {
                            autocompleteContainer.style.display = 'none';
                        }
                    }
                });
            }

            // Autocomplete
            if (searchInput && autocompleteContainer) {
                let searchTimeout = null;
                let currentResults = [];
                let selectedIndex = -1;

                searchInput.addEventListener('input', function(e) {
                    const query = e.target.value.trim();

                    clearTimeout(searchTimeout);

                    if (query.length < 3) {
                        autocompleteContainer.style.display = 'none';
                        currentResults = [];
                        return;
                    }

                    searchTimeout = setTimeout(function() {
                        fetch('/api/search/autocomplete?q=' + encodeURIComponent(query), {
                                headers: {
                                    'Accept': 'application/json',
                                    'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest'
                                }
                            })
                            .then(response => response.json())
                            .then(data => {
                                currentResults = data.results || [];
                                renderAutocomplete(currentResults);
                            })
                            .catch(error => {
                                console.error('Erro no autocomplete:', error);
                                autocompleteContainer.style.display = 'none';
                            });
                    }, 300);
                });

                searchInput.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
                    if (autocompleteContainer.style.display === 'none' || currentResults.length === 0) {
                        return;
                    }

                    if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
                        e.preventDefault();
                        selectedIndex = Math.min(selectedIndex + 1, currentResults.length - 1);
                        updateSelection();
                    } else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
                        e.preventDefault();
                        selectedIndex = Math.max(selectedIndex - 1, -1);
                        updateSelection();
                    } else if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                        e.preventDefault();
                        if (selectedIndex >= 0 && currentResults[selectedIndex]) {
                            window.location.href = currentResults[selectedIndex].url;
                        } else if (searchForm) {
                            searchForm.submit();
                        }
                    } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
                        autocompleteContainer.style.display = 'none';
                        selectedIndex = -1;
                    }
                });

                function renderAutocomplete(results) {
                    if (results.length === 0) {
                        autocompleteContainer.style.display = 'none';
                        return;
                    }

                    // Limitar a 3 resultados para exibição
                    const displayResults = results.slice(0, 3);

                    autocompleteContainer.innerHTML = '';
                    displayResults.forEach(function(result, index) {
                        const item = document.createElement('div');
                        item.className = 'search-autocomplete-item' + (result.image ? '' : ' no-image');
                        item.dataset.index = index;

                        let html = '';
                        if (result.image) {
                            html += '<img src="' + result.image +
                                '" alt="" class="search-autocomplete-item-image">';
                        }
                        html += '<div class="search-autocomplete-item-content">';
                        html += '<div class="search-autocomplete-item-title">' + escapeHtml(result.title) +
                            '</div>';
                        if (result.section) {
                            html += '<div class="search-autocomplete-item-section">' + escapeHtml(result
                                .section) + '</div>';
                        }
                        html += '</div>';

                        item.innerHTML = html;
                        item.addEventListener('click', function() {
                            window.location.href = result.url;
                        });
                        item.addEventListener('mouseenter', function() {
                            selectedIndex = index;
                            updateSelection();
                        });

                        autocompleteContainer.appendChild(item);
                    });

                    // Forçar exibição do dropdown
                    autocompleteContainer.style.display = 'block';
                    autocompleteContainer.style.visibility = 'visible';
                    autocompleteContainer.style.opacity = '1';
                    selectedIndex = -1;
                }

                function updateSelection() {
                    const items = autocompleteContainer.querySelectorAll('.search-autocomplete-item');
                    items.forEach(function(item, index) {
                        if (index === selectedIndex) {
                            item.classList.add('active');
                            item.scrollIntoView({
                                block: 'nearest',
                                behavior: 'smooth'
                            });
                        } else {
                            item.classList.remove('active');
                        }
                    });
                }

                function escapeHtml(text) {
                    const div = document.createElement('div');
                    div.textContent = text;
                    return div.innerHTML;
                }
            }
        })();
    </script>

    <script>
        (function() {
            'use strict';

            function initBannerVideos() {
                const videoWrappers = document.querySelectorAll('.banner-video-wrapper');

                videoWrappers.forEach(function(wrapper) {
                    const video = wrapper.querySelector('.banner-video');
                    const soundBtn = wrapper.querySelector('.banner-video-sound-btn');

                    if (!video || !soundBtn) return;

                    let isMuted = true;
                    let wasPlaying = false;

                    // Controle de som
                    soundBtn.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                        e.preventDefault();
                        e.stopPropagation();

                        isMuted = !isMuted;
                        video.muted = isMuted;

                        if (isMuted) {
                            soundBtn.classList.remove('active');
                        } else {
                            soundBtn.classList.add('active');
                            // Tentar tocar o vídeo quando ativar som
                            video.play().catch(function(err) {
                                console.log('Erro ao reproduzir vídeo:', err);
                            });
                        }
                    });

                    // Play/pause no hover
                    wrapper.addEventListener('mouseenter', function() {
                        wasPlaying = !video.paused;
                        if (!video.paused) {
                            video.pause();
                        }
                    });

                    wrapper.addEventListener('mouseleave', function() {
                        if (wasPlaying) {
                            video.play().catch(function(err) {
                                console.log('Erro ao reproduzir vídeo:', err);
                            });
                        }
                    });

                    // Pausar ao clicar no vídeo (se não tiver link)
                    const link = wrapper.querySelector('.banner-link');
                    if (!link) {
                        video.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                            e.preventDefault();
                            if (video.paused) {
                                video.play().catch(function(err) {
                                    console.log('Erro ao reproduzir vídeo:', err);
                                });
                            } else {
                                video.pause();
                            }
                        });
                    }
                });
            }

            // Inicializar quando o DOM estiver pronto
            if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
                document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initBannerVideos);
            } else {
                initBannerVideos();
            }

            // Re-inicializar para banners carregados dinamicamente
            const observer = new MutationObserver(function(mutations) {
                mutations.forEach(function(mutation) {
                    if (mutation.addedNodes.length) {
                        mutation.addedNodes.forEach(function(node) {
                            if (node.nodeType === 1 && (node.classList?.contains('banner-video-wrapper') || node.querySelector?.('.banner-video-wrapper'))) {
                                initBannerVideos();
                            }
                        });
                    }
                });
            });

            observer.observe(document.body, {
                childList: true,
                subtree: true
            });
        })();
    </script>

                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <!-- Navegação -->
                    <div class="story-navigation" style="display: flex !important; visibility: visible !important; opacity: 1 !important;">
                        <button class="story-nav-btn story-nav-prev" id="story-prev" aria-label="Anterior" style="display: flex !important; visibility: visible !important; opacity: 1 !important;">
                            <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="24" height="24" style="display: block;">
                                <path d="M15 18l-6-6 6-6" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" fill="none"></path>
                            </svg>
                        </button>
                        <button class="story-nav-btn story-nav-next" id="story-next" aria-label="Próximo" style="display: flex !important; visibility: visible !important; opacity: 1 !important;">
                            <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="24" height="24" style="display: block;">
                                <path d="M9 18l6-6-6-6" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" fill="none"></path>
                            </svg>
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>

<script>
(function() {
    let stories = [];
    let currentIndex = 0;
    let progressInterval = null;
    const storyDuration = 6000; // 6 segundos
    let isPressPaused = false;
    const overlay = document.getElementById('stories-overlay');
    const closeBtn = document.getElementById('stories-close');
    const loadingEl = document.getElementById('stories-loading');
    const emptyEl = document.getElementById('stories-empty');
    const contentEl = document.getElementById('stories-content');
    const storiesListEl = document.getElementById('stories-list');
    const storySlideEl = document.getElementById('story-slide');
    const storyImageEl = document.getElementById('story-image');
    const storyTitleEl = document.getElementById('story-title');
    const storyHeadlineEl = document.getElementById('story-headline');
    const storySectionEl = document.getElementById('story-section');
    const storyCounterEl = document.getElementById('story-counter');
    const storyLinkEl = document.getElementById('story-link');
    const progressEl = document.getElementById('story-progress');
    const prevBtn = document.getElementById('story-prev');
    const nextBtn = document.getElementById('story-next');

    function showLoading() {
        loadingEl.style.display = 'flex';
        emptyEl.style.display = 'none';
        contentEl.style.display = 'none';
    }

    function showEmpty() {
        loadingEl.style.display = 'none';
        emptyEl.style.display = 'flex';
        contentEl.style.display = 'none';
    }

    function showContent() {
        loadingEl.style.display = 'none';
        emptyEl.style.display = 'none';
        contentEl.style.display = 'flex';
    }

    function stopProgress() {
        if (progressInterval) {
            clearInterval(progressInterval);
            progressInterval = null;
        }
        if (progressEl) {
            progressEl.style.width = '0%';
        }
    }

    function startProgress() {
        stopProgress();
        if (!progressEl) return;
        
        let progress = 0;
        const increment = 100 / (storyDuration / 50);
        
        progressInterval = setInterval(function() {
            if (isPressPaused) {
                return;
            }
            progress += increment;
            progressEl.style.width = progress + '%';
            if (progress >= 100) {
                nextStory();
            }
        }, 50);
    }

    function pauseByPress() {
        isPressPaused = true;
    }

    function resumeByPress() {
        isPressPaused = false;
    }

    function loadStories() {
        showLoading();
        
        fetch('/api/stories?limit=15')
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                stories = (data.data || []).filter(story => story.image !== null);
                
                if (stories.length === 0) {
                    showEmpty();
                    return;
                }
                
                renderStoriesList();
                currentIndex = 0;
                showStory(0);
                showContent();
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('Erro ao carregar stories:', error);
                showEmpty();
            });
    }

    function renderStoriesList() {
        storiesListEl.innerHTML = '';
        stories.forEach(function(story, index) {
            const item = document.createElement('div');
            item.className = 'story-item' + (index === currentIndex ? ' active' : '');
            item.innerHTML = `
                <div class="story-item-avatar">
                    <img src="${story.image}" alt="${story.image_alt || story.title}" loading="lazy">
                </div>
                <div class="story-item-info">
                    <h4 class="story-item-title">${story.title}</h4>
                    <p class="story-item-section">${story.section || ''}</p>
                </div>
            `;
            item.addEventListener('click', function() {
                goToStory(index);
            });
            storiesListEl.appendChild(item);
        });
    }

    function showStory(index) {
        if (index < 0 || index >= stories.length) return;
        
        currentIndex = index;
        const story = stories[index];
        
        // Transição suave
        storySlideEl.style.transition = 'opacity 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1)';
        storySlideEl.style.opacity = '0';
        
        // Pré-carregar imagem para transição suave
        const img = new Image();
        img.onload = function() {
            setTimeout(function() {
                storyImageEl.src = story.image;
                storyImageEl.alt = story.image_alt || story.title;
                
                if (storyTitleEl) {
                    storyTitleEl.textContent = story.title;
                }
                
                if (storyHeadlineEl) {
                    storyHeadlineEl.textContent = story.headline || '';
                }
                
                if (storySectionEl) {
                    storySectionEl.textContent = story.section || '';
                    storySectionEl.style.display = story.section ? 'inline-block' : 'none';
                }
                
                if (storyCounterEl) {
                    storyCounterEl.textContent = `${index + 1} / ${stories.length}`;
                }
                
                if (storyLinkEl) {
                    storyLinkEl.href = story.url;
                }
                
                storySlideEl.style.display = 'flex';
                
                // Fade in suave
                requestAnimationFrame(function() {
                    storySlideEl.style.opacity = '1';
                });
            }, 100);
        };
        img.onerror = function() {
            // Se erro ao carregar, mostrar mesmo assim
            storyImageEl.src = story.image;
            storyImageEl.alt = story.image_alt || story.title;
            storySlideEl.style.display = 'flex';
            storySlideEl.style.opacity = '1';
        };
        img.src = story.image;
        
        // Atualizar lista
        const items = storiesListEl.querySelectorAll('.story-item');
        items.forEach(function(item, i) {
            if (i === index) {
                item.classList.add('active');
            } else {
                item.classList.remove('active');
            }
        });
        
        // Scroll suave para item ativo
        if (items[index]) {
            const item = items[index];
            const list = storiesListEl;
            const itemTop = item.offsetTop;
            const itemHeight = item.offsetHeight;
            const listTop = list.scrollTop;
            const listHeight = list.clientHeight;
            
            // Calcular posição ideal (centro da viewport)
            const targetScroll = itemTop - (listHeight / 2) + (itemHeight / 2);
            
            // Scroll suave
            list.scrollTo({
                top: targetScroll,
                behavior: 'smooth'
            });
        }
        
        // Atualizar navegação
        if (prevBtn) {
            prevBtn.disabled = index === 0;
        }
        if (nextBtn) {
            nextBtn.disabled = index === stories.length - 1;
        }
        
        startProgress();
    }

    function nextStory() {
        if (currentIndex < stories.length - 1) {
            showStory(currentIndex + 1);
        } else {
            closeStories();
        }
    }

    function previousStory() {
        if (currentIndex > 0) {
            showStory(currentIndex - 1);
        }
    }

    function goToStory(index) {
        showStory(index);
    }

    function openStories() {
        isPressPaused = false;
        overlay.style.display = 'flex';
        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        loadStories();
    }

    function closeStories() {
        isPressPaused = false;
        stopProgress();
        overlay.style.display = 'none';
        document.body.style.overflow = '';
        stories = [];
        currentIndex = 0;
    }

    // Event listeners
    if (closeBtn) {
        closeBtn.addEventListener('click', closeStories);
    }
    
    if (prevBtn) {
        prevBtn.addEventListener('click', previousStory);
    }
    
    if (nextBtn) {
        nextBtn.addEventListener('click', nextStory);
    }

    if (storySlideEl) {
        storySlideEl.addEventListener('touchstart', pauseByPress, { passive: true });
        storySlideEl.addEventListener('touchend', resumeByPress, { passive: true });
        storySlideEl.addEventListener('touchcancel', resumeByPress, { passive: true });
    }
    
    overlay.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        if (e.target === overlay) {
            closeStories();
        }
    });

    // Keyboard navigation
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
        if (overlay.style.display === 'none' || overlay.style.display === '') return;
        
        if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
            previousStory();
        } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
            nextStory();
        } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
            closeStories();
        }
    });

    // Expor função globalmente
    window.openStories = openStories;
})();
</script>

    
    <script>
        (function() {
            /**
             * Formata data no formato brasileiro: 23/02/2026 às 19h36
             */
            function formatBrazilianDateTime(date) {
                const day = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
                const month = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
                const year = date.getFullYear();
                const hours = String(date.getHours()).padStart(2, '0');
                const minutes = String(date.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');

                return `${day}/${month}/${year} às ${hours}h${minutes}`;
            }

            /**
             * Converte e atualiza uma data
             */
            function convertDate(element) {
                const datetime = element.getAttribute('data-datetime');
                if (!datetime) return;

                try {
                    // Parse da data ISO 8601 (UTC)
                    const utcDate = new Date(datetime);
                    if (isNaN(utcDate.getTime())) return;

                    // A data já será convertida automaticamente para o timezone local do navegador
                    const formatted = formatBrazilianDateTime(utcDate);

                    // Atualizar o texto
                    const dateText = element.querySelector('.date-text');
                    if (dateText) {
                        dateText.textContent = formatted;
                    } else {
                        // Se não tiver .date-text, atualizar o próprio elemento
                        element.textContent = formatted;
                    }
                } catch (e) {
                    console.warn('Erro ao converter data:', e);
                }
            }

            /**
             * Converte todas as datas na página
             */
            function convertAllDates() {
                const dateElements = document.querySelectorAll('[data-datetime]');
                dateElements.forEach(convertDate);
            }

            // Converter datas quando o DOM estiver pronto
            if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
                document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', convertAllDates);
            } else {
                convertAllDates();
            }

            // Observar mudanças dinâmicas (ex: carregar mais artigos)
            const observer = new MutationObserver(function(mutations) {
                mutations.forEach(function(mutation) {
                    mutation.addedNodes.forEach(function(node) {
                        if (node.nodeType === 1) { // Element node
                            // Converter datas no novo elemento
                            if (node.hasAttribute && node.hasAttribute('data-datetime')) {
                                convertDate(node);
                            }
                            // Converter datas nos filhos
                            const dateElements = node.querySelectorAll ? node.querySelectorAll('[data-datetime]') : [];
                            dateElements.forEach(convertDate);
                        }
                    });
                });
            });

            observer.observe(document.body, {
                childList: true,
                subtree: true
            });
        })();
    </script>

    <script>
        (function() {
            function runScripts(container) {
                container.querySelectorAll('script').forEach(function(oldScript) {
                    var newScript = document.createElement('script');
                    Array.from(oldScript.attributes).forEach(function(attr) {
                        newScript.setAttribute(attr.name, attr.value);
                    });
                    newScript.textContent = oldScript.textContent;
                    oldScript.parentNode.replaceChild(newScript, oldScript);
                });
            }

            /*
                Carrega o banner de cada slot.

                Dois cuidados que não estavam aqui e custaram banner no ar:

                1. O slot só é marcado como carregado DEPOIS de dar certo. Antes
                   a marca era posta antes do fetch e o erro era engolido por um
                   catch vazio: uma única requisição que falhasse (rede instável
                   de celular, 5xx momentâneo) deixava o espaço vazio para
                   sempre naquela página, sem nova tentativa. O leitor só via o
                   banner se recarregasse a tela — foi o sintoma relatado.

                2. Slot dentro de container escondido (display:none) não pede
                   nada. A matéria tem um rodapé só-de-celular que no computador
                   fica oculto, e ele pedia banner e contava impressão num
                   espaço que ninguém vê.
            */
            var TENTATIVAS = 3;

            function estaVisivel(el) {
                /*
                    Caminha os ancestrais procurando display:none.

                    A primeira versão usava offsetParent/getClientRects, e não
                    funcionou: na varredura do DOMContentLoaded o layout ainda
                    não está resolvido, e o slot só-de-celular (dentro de
                    .mobile-related-articles) passava pelo teste no computador.
                    getComputedStyle não depende de layout pronto.
                */
                for (var n = el; n && n !== document.documentElement; n = n.parentElement) {
                    var cs = window.getComputedStyle(n);
                    if (cs.display === 'none') { return false; }
                }
                return true;
            }

            function pedirBanner(el, tentativa) {
                var region = el.getAttribute('data-banner-region');

                var params = new URLSearchParams();
                params.set('region_code', region);
                var sectionId = el.getAttribute('data-banner-section');
                if (sectionId) params.set('section_id', sectionId);
                params.set('is_homepage', el.getAttribute('data-banner-homepage') === '1' ? '1' : '0');

                fetch('/api/banners/render?' + params.toString())
                    .then(function (r) {
                        if (!r.ok) { throw new Error('HTTP ' + r.status); }
                        return r.json();
                    })
                    .then(function (data) {
                        if (data && data.html) {
                            el.innerHTML = data.html;
                            runScripts(el);
                        }
                        // Sem html é resposta legítima (região sem banner):
                        // marca como resolvido para não ficar repetindo.
                        el.setAttribute('data-banner-loaded', '1');
                    })
                    .catch(function () {
                        if (tentativa < TENTATIVAS) {
                            setTimeout(function () { pedirBanner(el, tentativa + 1); }, tentativa * 800);
                            return;
                        }
                        // Esgotou: libera o slot para uma próxima varredura
                        // (troca de orientação, volta de aba) em vez de
                        // cravá-lo como carregado.
                        el.removeAttribute('data-banner-pedindo');
                    });
            }

            function loadBannerPlaceholders() {
                document.querySelectorAll('.banner-placeholder:not([data-banner-loaded]):not([data-banner-pedindo])').forEach(function (el) {
                    if (!el.getAttribute('data-banner-region')) return;
                    if (!estaVisivel(el)) return;
                    el.setAttribute('data-banner-pedindo', '1');
                    pedirBanner(el, 1);
                });
            }

            if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
                document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', loadBannerPlaceholders);
            } else {
                loadBannerPlaceholders();
            }

            // Slot que ficou sem banner (falhou, ou estava escondido na carga)
            // ganha nova chance quando a aba volta ou a tela muda de tamanho.
            document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () {
                if (!document.hidden) { loadBannerPlaceholders(); }
            });
            window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
                clearTimeout(window.__phBannerResize);
                window.__phBannerResize = setTimeout(loadBannerPlaceholders, 300);
            });
        })();
    </script>
